- **kosovo-income-tax**: Compute Kosovo personal income tax for 2026 individuals, sole traders and partners from the current Personal Income Tax Law and its amendments. - **kosovo-vat**: Use this skill whenever asked to prepare, review, or classify transactions for a Kosovo VAT (TVSH) return for any client. Trigger on phrases like "Kosovo VAT", "Kosovo TVSH", "TAK filing", or any request involving Kosovo VAT. This skill covers standard TVSH payers filing monthly returns.
